exp_14b_inference_fewnerd 结果收集\n\n### 4. 📋 待办\n- worker_collect 回调 → 如果 FewNERD 14B 结果完整：update_registry(conclusion=success, metrics=...) + 汇报 Director scaling curve 数据点\n- worker_collect 回调 → 如果进程已死无结果：重新提交 14B inference（用正确 PID 监控 + enforce_eager）\n- worker_gpu_5090c_fill 回调 → 确认 5090-c 空闲卡已填满（cuda:0/2/3）\n- exp_qwen25_32b_fewnerd cron 完成 → 分析 32B FewNERD 结果，与 7B/14B 对比 scaling law\n- B1 SCD 三实验 cron 完成 → on-experiment-done 流程，与 D193 CoNLL negative control 对比\n- ESJ 完成 → entity-level self-judgment 结果分析，对标 majority vote baseline\n\n### 5. 🎯 战略上下文\n- **项目核心**: Self-Consistency for NER，ceiling-gap diagnostic → prescriptive framework\n- **当前阶段**: R26 审稿 3.5/3.0，双线并行 Track A (writing v3) + Track B (method breakthrough: E_θ/ESJ/SCD/14B-32B scaling)\n- **关键决策链**: D197(双线并行) → D198(pivot to prescriptive) → D199(reverse conservative) → D200(E_θ V1 negative) → D201(entity-level 全 negative, pivot to sampling-level)\n- **易错点**: \n  - autodl-5090-b 的 B1 SCD 实验没有 gpu_ids 记录在 registry，resource-usage 显示 idle 但实际在跑——不要重复提交到这些卡\n  - 14B inference 脚本 run_mvp_pilot.py 跑多数据集（SciERC/CoNLL/FewNERD）但跨域结果无意义，只有 FewNERD 有价值\n  - cron 对 14B inference 会反复触发 failed 通知（已是 stale），忽略即可\n  - 32B 训练完成后下一步是 32B inference（同 run_mvp_pilot.py），需要 enforce_eager + 正确 PID 注册\n\n## 可查的历史\n前 43 轮 handoff 在 `task_context/history/agent/loop_*.md`，如有疑问可 Read 回查。\n完整聊天归档可用 `recall_session_history(\"关键词\")` 搜索。\n\n## 第一个动作要求\n1. 用 `read_registry` 确认当前 RUNNING/PREPARING 实验状态（防止幻觉记忆过时状态）\n2. 回复一句话确认你看到了上面的硬约束 + 当前活跃实验，再开始任何工具调用"

[HANDOFF] 你是 Main Agent，这是 Loop #17。

## 上一轮交接 (task_context)

### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
- GPU 白名单：autodl-5090 cuda:0-3, autodl-5090-b cuda:0-3, autodl-5090-c cuda:0-3（共12张）
- autodl-5090-c:cuda:0 上有 14B inference 进程（PID 217692），可能已完成但未确认——不要在 cuda:0 上启动新任务前先确认进程状态
- B1 SCD 实验跑在 autodl-5090-b（PID 207109/205634/205991），reconcile 确认存活——不要 kill
- exp_14b_inference_fewnerd 被 cron 误标为 failed（PID mismatch），实际进程在跑或已完成——registry 状态需修正
- 14B 模型推理需 enforce_eager=True 否则 CUDA graph OOM（教训已写入 memory.md）

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
- **autodl-5090**: 4×RTX 5090, 工作目录 /root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie, conda base (/root/miniconda3)
- **autodl-5090-b**: 4×RTX 5090, 同上路径结构, B1 SCD 三实验在跑
- **autodl-5090-c**: 4×RTX 5090, 同上路径结构, 14B merged model at /root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/checkpoints/qwen25-14b-fewnerd-merged-final
- **32B FT**: autodl-5090 全4卡, exp_qwen25_32b_fewnerd (PID 804438, cron active)
- **ESJ**: autodl-5090-c:cuda:1, exp_esj_entity_self_judgment (PID 218678)
- **磁盘充裕**: 5090 813G free, 5090-b 427G free, 5090-c 813G free (根分区)

### 3. 📊 当前状态
**刚做完**: session-start 初始化 + 处理 exp_14b_inference_fewnerd 失败回调（诊断发现是 PID mismatch 误报）

**活跃 Workers**（5个）:
- worker_collect_exp_14b_inference_fewnerd_results — 去 5090-c 收集 14B FewNERD 推理结果
- worker_gpu_5090c_fill_4gpu — 向 5090-c 空闲 GPU 提交新实验
- worker_cpu_failure_gallery — CPU 任务
- worker_cpu_alpha_crit_derivation — CPU 任务
- worker_gpu_5090_check_and_fill — 检查 5090 并填充

**RUNNING 实验**:
- exp_qwen25_32b_fewnerd (5090 ×4卡, ~73% 完成)
- exp_b1_scd_fewnerd_standard (5090-b, PID 207109)
- exp_b1_scd_fewnerd_curriculum_q (5090-b, PID 205634)
- exp_b1_scd_threshold_ablation (5090-b, PID 205991)
- exp_e_theta_adaptive (local-cpu)
- exp_esj_entity_self_judgment (5090-c:cuda:1)

**等待**: 5个 Worker 回调; exp_14b_inference_fewnerd 结果收集

### 4. 📋 待办
- worker_collect 回调 → 如果 FewNERD 14B 结果完整：update_registry(conclusion=success, metrics=...) + 汇报 Director scaling curve 数据点
- worker_collect 回调 → 如果进程已死无结果：重新提交 14B inference（用正确 PID 监控 + enforce_eager）
- worker_gpu_5090c_fill 回调 → 确认 5090-c 空闲卡已填满（cuda:0/2/3）
- exp_qwen25_32b_fewnerd cron 完成 → 分析 32B FewNERD 结果，与 7B/14B 对比 scaling law
- B1 SCD 三实验 cron 完成 → on-experiment-done 流程，与 D193 CoNLL negative control 对比
- ESJ 完成 → entity-level self-judgment 结果分析，对标 majority vote baseline

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
- **项目核心**: Self-Consistency for NER，ceiling-gap diagnostic → prescriptive framework
- **当前阶段**: R26 审稿 3.5/3.0，双线并行 Track A (writing v3) + Track B (method breakthrough: E_θ/ESJ/SCD/14B-32B scaling)
- **关键决策链**: D197(双线并行) → D198(pivot to prescriptive) → D199(reverse conservative) → D200(E_θ V1 negative) → D201(entity-level 全 negative, pivot to sampling-level)
- **易错点**: 
  - autodl-5090-b 的 B1 SCD 实验没有 gpu_ids 记录在 registry，resource-usage 显示 idle 但实际在跑——不要重复提交到这些卡
  - 14B inference 脚本 run_mvp_pilot.py 跑多数据集（SciERC/CoNLL/FewNERD）但跨域结果无意义，只有 FewNERD 有价值
  - cron 对 14B inference 会反复触发 failed 通知（已是 stale），忽略即可
  - 32B 训练完成后下一步是 32B inference（同 run_mvp_pilot.py），需要 enforce_eager + 正确 PID 注册

    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

\"hypothesis\": \"Heterogeneous-Temperature Sampling: MRSC/LSC negative results prove entity-level correction cannot close the selection gap. F4 degeneracy analysis shows T=0.7 has ~62% degeneracy (too homogeneous) while T=1.0 has more diversity but noisier entities. Mixing samples from different temperatures (k from T_low + (8-k) from T_high) breaks temperature-specific degeneracy patterns: low-T samples provide precise entities, high-T samples provide diversity. Mixed-T N=8 pool should achieve lower degeneracy than pure T_low and higher construction F1 than pure T_high. Hypothesis source: entity-level methods (MRSC weighted re-prompting, LSC latent aggregation) both failed → pivot to sampling-level diversity improvement.\", \"parent\": \"exp_mrsc_multi_round\", \"metrics\": {\"t08_vs_t10\": {\"seed42\": {\"pure_t_low_f1\": 0.6755, \"pure_t_high_f1\": 0.6643, \"best_mix\": \"5:3\", \"best_mix_f1\": 0.6708, \"delta_vs_low\": -0.0046, \"delta_vs_high\": 0.0066}, \"seed123\": {\"pure_t_low_f1\": 0.6659, \"pure_t_high_f1\": 0.6681, \"best_mix\": \"6:2\", \"best_mix_f1\": 0.6655, \"delta_vs_low\": -0.0004, \"delta_vs_high\": -0.0026}, \"seed456\": {\"pure_t_low_f1\": 0.6675, \"pure_t_high_f1\": 0.6663, \"best_mix\": \"4:4\", \"best_mix_f1\": 0.6654, \"delta_vs_low\": -0.002, \"delta_vs_high\": -0.0008}}, \"t05_vs_t10\": {\"seed42\": {\"pure_t_low_f1\": 0.663, \"pure_t_high_f1\": 0.6643, \"best_mix\": \"5:3\", \"best_mix_f1\": 0.6654, \"delta_vs_low\": 0.0024, \"delta_vs_high\": 0.0011}, \"seed123\": {\"pure_t_low_f1\": 0.6665, \"pure_t_high_f1\": 0.6681, \"best_mix\": \"2:6\", \"best_mix_f1\": 0.6671, \"delta_vs_low\": 0.0006, \"delta_vs_high\": -0.001}, \"seed456\": {\"pure_t_low_f1\": 0.6686, \"pure_t_high_f1\": 0.6663, \"best_mix\": \"4:4\", \"best_mix_f1\": 0.6682, \"delta_vs_low\": -0.0004, \"delta_vs_high\": 0.002}}, \"degeneracy\": {\"t05_mean\": 0.356, \"t08_mean\": 0.178, \"t10_mean\": 0.126}, \"max_delta_any_combo\": 0.0066}, \"conclusion\": \"negative\", \"notes\": \"Heterogeneous temperature mixing provides no consistent F1 improvement over pure single-temperature sampling on SciERC (551 instances, 3 seeds, 2 temp pairs). All F1 deltas within ±0.005, inconsistent direction across seeds. Mixing does reduce degeneracy (T=0.5: 35%→17%) but this doesn't translate to F1 gain. The pure T_low vs T_high F1 gap is already tiny (~0.001-0.01), leaving no room for mixing to exploit. Joins MRSC/LSC as evidence that sampling-level interventions cannot improve construction F1 when the bottleneck is not sample diversity.\"}, \"exp_a1_spider_sql_sc\": {\"status\": \"PREPARING\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-20T18:15:12.865Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-20T18:15:33.104Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"Cross-task universality: SC diagnostic cascade (degeneracy, LP-quality gap, agreement-correctness correlation) applies to SQL structured generation on Spider. D176: appendix-only, not main claim.\"}, \"exp_pareto_diversity_quality\": {\"status\": \"PREPARING\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-20T09:31:45.421Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-20T09:31:58.776Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"There exists a Pareto frontier in the diversity (1-degeneracy) vs construction quality (ΔF1) space across temperature configurations. Well-separated temperature mixing (T=0.7+T=1.0) achieves Pareto optimality for both uniform and VC construction, while VC-weighted construction flattens the trade-off slope, reducing the diversity cost of quality.\", \"metrics\": {\"dataset\": \"SciERC\", \"configs\": {\"t07\": {\"one_minus_degen\": 0.777, \"uniform_delta\": 1.81, \"vc_delta\": 1.17, \"pareto\": \"uniform only\"}, \"t08\": {\"one_minus_degen\": 0.819, \"uniform_delta\": 1.25, \"vc_delta\": 1.68, \"pareto\": \"VC only\"}, \"mix_07_10\": {\"one_minus_degen\": 0.83, \"uniform_delta\": 1.43, \"vc_delta\": 1.67, \"pareto\": \"both\"}, \"t10\": {\"one_minus_degen\": 0.877, \"uniform_delta\": 0.75, \"vc_delta\": 1.21, \"pareto\": \"both (highest diversity)\"}, \"mix_08_10\": {\"one_minus_degen\": 0.789, \"uniform_delta\": -0.17, \"vc_delta\": 0.47, \"pareto\": \"dominated\"}}, \"vc_slope_reduction\": \"~50% flatter than uniform\", \"key_insight\": \"well-separated T mixing dual Pareto optimal; close T mixing dominated\"}, \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"notes\": \"A4 Pareto diversity-quality analysis on SciERC. Mix T=0.7+T=1.0 is dual-Pareto optimal (uniform +1.43pp, VC +1.67pp). VC construction flattens trade-off slope ~50% vs uniform — increasing diversity costs less quality. Mix T=0.8+T=1.0 is dominated (close T → higher degeneracy, no construction gain). Confirms DGEC heterogeneous-T finding: ΔT matters, ΔT=0.3 beneficial, ΔT=0.2 harmful. Pareto plot artifact registered.\"}, \"exp_adaptive_sampling_budget\": {\"status\": \"PREPARING\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-20T09:29:22.761Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-20T09:29:41.158Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"Confidence-aware adaptive sampling budget allocation (fewer samples for high-confidence instances, full N=8 for low-confidence) can recover nearly all SC F1 gains while saving 40-50% of inference cost, because high-confidence instances already have low diversity and gain nothing from additional samples.\", \"metrics\": {\"model\": \"Qwen3-8B FT\", \"fewnerd\": {\"n_instances\": 37648, \"greedy_f1\": 0.7884, \"uniform_n8_f1\": 0.7819, \"best_adaptive\": \"h1_l8\", \"best_adaptive_f1\": 0.7849, \"best_delta_vs_u8\": \"+0.30pp\", \"sample_savings\": \"43.8%\", \"note\": \"Greedy > U8; adaptive h1/l8 best: greedy for high-conf, N=8 SC for low-conf\"}, \"scierc\": {\"n_instances\": 551, \"greedy_f1\": 0.6592, \"uniform_n8_f1\": 0.6712, \"best_adaptive\": \"top67%_h2_l8\", \"best_adaptive_f1\": 0.6707, \"best_delta_vs_u8\": \"-0.05pp\", \"sample_savings\": \"50.2%\", \"note\": \"U8 > Greedy; top67% h2/l8 saves 50% with negligible loss\"}}, \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"notes\": \"B7 adaptive sampling budget analysis on Qwen3-8B FT. Key findings: (1) FewNERD: greedy already beats uniform N=8 SC (+0.65pp), adaptive h1/l8 is optimal (+0.30pp vs U8, saves 43.8% samples); (2) SciERC: U8 beats greedy (+1.20pp), top67% h2/l8 saves 50% with only -0.05pp loss. Practical implication: confidence-gated sampling can halve inference cost. Router ΔF1 should exceed always-LP ΔF1.\"}, \"exp_pareto_diversity_quality\": {\"hypothesis\": \"There exists a Pareto frontier in the diversity (1-degeneracy) vs construction quality (ΔF1) space across temperature configurations. Well-separated temperature mixing (T=0.7+T=1.0) achieves Pareto optimality for both uniform and VC construction, while VC-weighted construction flattens the trade-off slope, reducing the diversity cost of quality.\"}, \"exp_adaptive_sampling_budget\": {\"hypothesis\": \"Confidence-aware adaptive sampling budget allocation (fewer samples for high-confidence instances, full N=8 for low-confidence) can recover nearly all SC F1 gains while saving 40-50% of inference cost, because high-confidence instances already have low diversity and gain nothing from additional samples.\"}, \"exp_dgec_sj_construction\": {\"hypothesis\": \"SJ-weighted entity construction serves as control for VC — if SJ (soft entity overlap) works as well as VC (exact match consensus), any stronger QE signal suffices for construction; if VC specifically outperforms SJ, exact-match consensus has a unique advantage over soft similarity for entity-level selection.\"}, \"exp_dgec_vc_construction\": {\"hypothesis\": \"VC-weighted entity construction outperforms greedy and LP-weighted construction across configs because Vote Count is a more direct quality estimator for structured outputs — it measures exact-match consensus without relying on model-internal log probabilities. Especially effective in high-degeneracy regimes where LP compression makes LP-weighting uninformative.\"}, \"exp_dgec_heterogeneous_t\": {\"hypothesis\": \"Mixing samples from T=0.7 and T=1.0 significantly reduces degeneracy compared to single-T sampling (by breaking temperature-induced mode collapse), enabling entity construction to achieve significant ΔF1 improvement even in configs where single-T construction was n.s. due to high degeneracy.\"}}, \"count\": 18, \"total\": 18, \"baseline\": null}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.7725}}"}

+0.1054 | +0.0890 |\\n| Fleiss κ (mean) | 0.4501 | 0.5470 |\\n| Soft Jaccard (mean) | 0.6881 | 0.7764 |\\n| Sample F1 (mean±std) | 0.523±0.332 | 0.581±0.384 |\\n| Sample F1=0 (%) | 18.2% | 23.4% |\\n| Full ρ(κ,F1) | +0.383 | +0.377 |\\n| Full ρ(SJ,F1) | +0.433 | +0.392 |\\n| Cond ρ(κ,F1) | +0.371 | +0.435 |\\n| Cond ρ(SJ,F1) | +0.440 | +0.467 |\\n| Structural > Surface | YES (+0.050) | YES (+0.015) |\\n| LP full ρ | +0.189 | -0.020 (ns) |\\n| LP full AUROC | 0.596 | 0.538 |\\n| LP cond ρ | +0.176 | +0.210 |\\n| LP cond AUROC | 0.593 | 0.580 |\\n| Verdict | PASS | MARGINAL |\\n\\n### Conclusion: SUCCESS\\n\\n**Correlation-selection gap confirmed on pretrained model.**\\n\\n1. **Gap exists**: Oracle F1 (0.618) exceeds Greedy F1 (0.512) by 10.5pp — substantial untapped selection potential in the 8 samples\\n2. **Agreement correlates with correctness**: ρ(SJ,F1) = +0.43 (p≈0), but this correlation does NOT close the gap via naive MV → classic correlation-selection gap\\n3. **Structural > Surface**: Soft Jaccard beats Fleiss κ as quality signal by +0.050 ρ — consistent across both datasets\\n4. **Logprob weak**: LP correlation exists (ρ=0.19) but much weaker than agreement-based signals — LP alone insufficient for selection\\n5. **Cross-dataset consistency**: Gap pattern replicates across FewNERD (37.6K instances) and CoNLL (3.5K instances), with FewNERD showing larger headroom and stronger structural advantage\\n\\n**Implication**: The gap is model-intrinsic (not a fine-tuning artifact). Pretrained Qwen2.5-7B already exhibits the phenomenon where agreement signals predict quality but standard aggregation fails to exploit this — motivating learned selection/weighting approaches.\\n\\n## Pretrained MV — 3/3 datasets positive (2026-05-21)\\n\\nPretrained Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct MV strict is **positive on all 3 datasets**:\\n- FewNERD: MV Δ = +3.90±0.22pp, degen=11.7%, α_crit=0.356±0.088 (3-seed)\\n- SciERC: MV Δ = +1.54±0.48pp, degen=0.8%, α_crit=0.151±0.004 (3-seed)\\n- CoNLL: MV positive (待汇总精确数字)\\n\\nFinetuned MV is negative on all 3 datasets. This pretrained-vs-finetuned contrast is a core narrative element.\\n\\n## 5-epoch Epoch Confound Analysis (2026-05-21)\\n\\n### 3-epoch vs 5-epoch FewNERD (finetuned)\\n\\n| 指标 | 3-epoch | 5-epoch (3-seed Mean±Std) |\\n|------|---------|--------------------------|\\n| greedy_f1 | 0.7969 | 0.7978±0.0002 |\\n| mv_strict_f1 | 0.7695 | 0.7942±0.0008 |\\n| MV Δ | -2.74pp | -0.36±0.09pp |\\n| degeneracy | 8.8% | 26.2±0.7% |\\n\\n**关键发现**: 5-epoch degeneracy 飙升至 26%（3倍于 3-epoch），采样多样性严重坍塌。MV Δ 表面收窄但是 MV≈greedy 的退化效果。Greedy 基本不变。\\n\\n**叙事链**: pretrained(degen 11.7%, MV +3.9pp) → 3-epoch(degen 8.8%, MV -2.74pp) → 5-epoch(degen 26.2%, MV -0.36pp)\\n\\n**注意**: 3-epoch degen(8.8%) < pretrained degen(11.7%) — 非单调！Fine-tuning 初期降低 degeneracy（因为模型变得更确信但输出仍有变化），5-epoch 才导致坍塌。叙事需处理这个非单调性。\\n\\n## Per-type FewNERD Analysis (2026-05-21)\\n\\n**θ 依赖性是关键 — 之前报告的 +0.90 仅在 strict MV 下成立：**\\n\\n| 配置 | θ | Pearson r | p-value |\\n|------|---|-----------|---------|\\n| exp_021 (3-epoch) | 0.5 (strict) | **+0.87** | **0.005** |\\n| mf4v2 (3-epoch) | 0.5 (strict) | +0.77 | 0.024 |\\n| exp_021 | 0.25 (liberal) | -0.52 | 0.19 (ns) |\\n\\n**机制**: strict MV (θ=0.5) 是强过滤器，easy types 样本一致性高→MV 保留大部分实体；hard types 样本分散→MV 丢弃过多→F1 下降大。Liberal MV (θ=0.25) 反而让 hard types 受益（聚合多样正确实体）。\\n\\n**含义**: 排除了 \\\"baseline quality confound\\\"——strict MV 下，高 greedy_f1 实体类型 MV loss 更大（非更小），符合直觉。\\n\\nArtifact: `art_20260521_81fcee` (fewnerd_per_type_mv_analysis)\\nServer JSON: `/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/output/fewnerd_per_type_mv_analysis.json`\\n\", \"total_chars\": 3856, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}

This pretrained-vs-finetuned contrast is a core narrative element.\\n\\n## 5-epoch Epoch Confound Analysis (2026-05-21)\\n\\n### 3-epoch vs 5-epoch FewNERD (finetuned)\\n\\n| 指标 | 3-epoch | 5-epoch (3-seed Mean±Std) |\\n|------|---------|--------------------------|\\n| greedy_f1 | 0.7969 | 0.7978±0.0002 |\\n| mv_strict_f1 | 0.7695 | 0.7942±0.0008 |\\n| MV Δ | -2.74pp | -0.36±0.09pp |\\n| degeneracy | 8.8% | 26.2±0.7% |\\n\\n**关键发现**: 5-epoch degeneracy 飙升至 26%（3倍于 3-epoch），采样多样性严重坍塌。MV Δ 表面收窄但是 MV≈greedy 的退化效果。Greedy 基本不变。\\n\\n**叙事链**: pretrained(degen 11.7%, MV +3.9pp) → 3-epoch(degen 8.8%, MV -2.74pp) → 5-epoch(degen 26.2%, MV -0.36pp)\\n\\n**注意**: 3-epoch degen(8.8%) < pretrained degen(11.7%) — 非单调！Fine-tuning 初期降低 degeneracy（因为模型变得更确信但输出仍有变化），5-epoch 才导致坍塌。叙事需处理这个非单调性。\\n\\n## Per-type FewNERD Analysis (2026-05-21)\\n\\n**θ 依赖性是关键 — 之前报告的 +0.90 仅在 strict MV 下成立：**\\n\\n| 配置 | θ | Pearson r | p-value |\\n|------|---|-----------|---------|\\n| exp_021 (3-epoch) | 0.5 (strict) | **+0.87** | **0.005** |\\n| mf4v2 (3-epoch) | 0.5 (strict) | +0.77 | 0.024 |\\n| exp_021 | 0.25 (liberal) | -0.52 | 0.19 (ns) |\\n\\n**机制**: strict MV (θ=0.5) 是强过滤器，easy types 样本一致性高→MV 保留大部分实体；hard types 样本分散→MV 丢弃过多→F1 下降大。Liberal MV (θ=0.25) 反而让 hard types 受益（聚合多样正确实体）。\\n\\n**含义**: 排除了 \\\"baseline quality confound\\\"——strict MV 下，高 greedy_f1 实体类型 MV loss 更大（非更小），符合直觉。\\n\\nArtifact: `art_20260521_81fcee` (fewnerd_per_type_mv_analysis)\\nServer JSON: `/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/output/fewnerd_per_type_mv_analysis.json`\\n\", \"total_chars\": 3856, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"
    }

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01AivyX1zrjv4KheQ3cMTGj8`</summary>

```text
1	---
2	name: resource-saturation
3	description: "当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。"
4	always: false
5	roles:
6	  - director
7	  - agent
8	---
9	
10	# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）
11	
12	## A. GPU 分配率检查
13	
14	调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:
15	- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号
16	- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）
17	- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️
18	
19	任何"分配了但没跑"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:
20	- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备
21	- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）
22	- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报"暂无可铺实验"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作
23	
24	**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。
25	
26	## B. GPU 效率检查
27	
28	调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:
29	- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务
30	- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）
31	- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优
32	
33	## C. 非 GPU 任务并行
34	
35	GPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:
36	- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint
37	- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置
38	- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索
39	- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合
40	- 论文章节起草
41	- 代码 review / refactor
42	
43	**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。
44	
45	## D. 汇报格式
46	
47	向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:
48	
49	    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)
50	
```
